上銀(2049) 
買進, TP 450 (潛在報酬 54.37%)  
買進 
Price(11/10)：291.5 
Target Price：450

26Q3 受惠終端需求強勁，營收達 81.10 億元，QoQ 8.01%，YoY 
35.27%；受惠稼動率提升與價格策略帶動毛利走高，單季 EPS 達
3.48 元。10 月營收延續強勁動能，達 28.50 億元，MoM 4.79%，
YoY 35.66%，累計前 10 月營收為 248.48 億元，YoY 25.11%。
目前整體市場供不應求，訂單能見度已直達 2027 年中。 
展望後市，隨公司下半年增聘逾 300 名員工並持續加班生產，26
年底產出量將較年初成長 25%至 30%，預期 26Q4 將打破歐美長
假與盤點淡季效應，營運有望優於 26Q3。研究部預估 26Q4 營收
為 85.10 億元，QoQ 4.94%，YoY 30.86%；毛利率估為 35.11%，
營業利益 12.53 億元，QoQ -13.32%，YoY 297.33%；單季 EPS
為 2.95 元。綜合考量下游客戶需求持續暢旺、公司優先承接高毛
利訂單使獲利結構性優化，並已長期佈局機器人與 CPO 商機，研
究部預估 26 與 27 年營收為 305.08 億元與 344.40 億元，YoY 
25.74%與 12.89%；毛利率為 34.54%與 35.11%；營業利益為 43.63
億元與 58.60 億元，YoY 165.52%與 34.30%；稅後獲利為 36.93
億元與 48.07 億元，YoY 142.05%與 30.15%；預估 26 與 27 年
EPS 分別為 10.44 元與 13.59 元。 
評價方面，上銀過去五年本益比主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間。
考量公司整體營收與獲利將呈現雙位數之成長上升趨勢，且自動化
與傳動產業結構持續復甦上行，研究部以 2027 年預估 EPS 13.59
元作為評價基礎，並給予歷史區間中緣偏上之 33 倍 PER，目標價
設定為 450 元，給予買進之投資建議。     
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
上銀科技(2049)成立於 1989 年，主要從事線性滑軌(GW)、滾
螺桿(BS)、工業機器人、機器手臂、迴轉工作台、減速機、精
密軸承及醫療設備之研究開發、設計、製造、銷售、維修及售
前售後服務，同時為全球第三大滾珠螺桿廠和線性滑軌廠，僅
次於日本 THK、NSK。公司以自有品牌「HIWIN」行銷全球，
海外子公司橫跨德國、美國、日本、瑞士、捷克、法國，

⚫ 26Q3 EPS 為 3.48 元，機器人營收年成長 8 成 
營收 81.10 億，QoQ 8.01%，YoY 35.27%，除歐洲市場外，
其餘市場皆呈現年紀雙成長，其中機器人相關產品年增 82%。 
獲利表現方面，毛利率 36.34%，季增 2.18 個百分點，年增 
7.74 個百分點，提升主要因(1)漲價效益浮現、(2)UTR 提升、

⚫ 26Q4 有望淡季不淡，估營收季增 4.94%達 85.10 億 
上銀 10 月營收 28.50，YoY 35.66%，MoM4.79%，累計
營收為 248.48 億， YoY25.11%。目前下游需求仍強勁，政
體市場處供不應求之態勢，隨 26H2 增聘逾 300 名員工並採
加班生產，26 年底產出量較年初成長 25%～30%，預期 
26Q4 有望突破歐美長假與盤點淡季效應，營運有望優於 
26Q3。 
研究部預估，26Q4 營收 85.10 億，QoQ 4.94%，YoY 30.86%；
毛利率 35.11%，季減 1.23 個百分點，年增 8.07 個百分點；
營業利益 12.53 億，QoQ -13.32%，YoY 297.33%；稅後獲
利 10.45 億，QoQ -14.42%，YoY 126.22%；EPS 為 2.95 元。

⚫ 訂單滿載，能見度達 2027 年中 
估計 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 10.44/13.59 元。 
目前下游需求仍呈現供不應求，研磨級滾珠螺桿交期已拉長至 
6.5 個月以上，線性滑軌依產品規格亦維持 4～6 個月，整體
訂單能見度直達 2027 年中，且 26Q3 合約負債達 1.34 億
元（YoY +126%）。 
各區域表現方面，亞洲市場受惠中國大陸工具機產業升級，帶
動傳動元件需求持續成長，同時台灣與亞洲各國受惠半導體製
造需求熱絡，推升機器人、馬達與旋轉轉台等零組件出貨；美
國受惠製造業回流、半導體擴產與 AI 資料中心建置潮，帶動
強勁設備需求；歐洲雖受車市疲軟影響而整體持平，但軍工與
潛在戰後重建需求已逐步浮現。 
獲利與產能方面，管理層表示現階段不傾向全面漲價，而採取
融資率 4.25 -0.09 
券餘(張) 57 23

「擇優挑單」之訂單組合優化策略，優先承接高獲利訂單以帶

水準，聚焦於設備去瓶頸與自動化汰換。

⚫ 長期佈局機器人與 CPO 商機 
機器人業務佔 26Q3 營收達 12%，其中 EFEM 隨半導體需
求熱絡穩健成長，並已超前部署對應 FOPLP 方片規格之移載
系統。公司充分發揮垂直整合優勢，自製減速機、螺桿、軸承，
並整合關係企業大銀微系統之伺服馬達與驅動器。目前已獲美
系人形機器人廠商小批量線性滑軌訂單，美系 AI 物流機器人
放量時程則取決於客戶端實測進度。CPO 方面，大銀微已開
發出奈米級定位平台，並整合上銀微小型螺桿與線軌，共同搶
攻次世代半導體設備商機。

⚫ 結論與建議: 
26Q3 受惠終端需求強勁，營收達 81.10 億元，QoQ 8.01%，
YoY 35.27%；受惠稼動率提升與價格策略帶動毛利走高，單
季 EPS 達 3.48 元。10 月營收延續強勁動能，達 28.50 億元，
MoM 4.79%，YoY 35.66%，累計前 10 月營收為 248.48 億元，
YoY 25.11%。目前整體市場供不應求，訂單能見度已直達
2027 年中。    
展望後市，隨公司下半年增聘逾 300 名員工並持續加班生產，
26 年底產出量將較年初成長 25%至 30%，預期 26Q4 將打破
歐美長假與盤點淡季效應，營運有望優於 26Q3。研究部預估
26Q4 營收為 85.10 億元，QoQ 4.94%，YoY 30.86%；毛利
率估為 35.11%，營業利益 12.53 億元，QoQ -13.32%，YoY 
297.33%；單季 EPS 為 2.95 元。    
綜合考量下游客戶需求持續暢旺、公司優先承接高毛利訂單使
獲利結構性優化，並已長期佈局機器人與 CPO 商機，研究部
預估 26 與 27 年營收為 305.08 億元與 344.40 億元，YoY 
25.74%與 12.89%；毛利率為 34.54%與 35.11%；營業利益
為 43.63 億元與 58.60 億元，YoY 165.52%與 34.30%；稅後
獲利為 36.93 億元與 48.07 億元，YoY 142.05%與 30.15%；
預估 26 與 27 年 EPS 分別為 10.44 元與 13.59 元。    
評價方面，上銀過去五年本益比主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區
間。考量公司整體營收與獲利將呈現雙位數之成長上升趨勢，
且自動化與傳動產業結構持續復甦上行，研究部以 2027 年預
估 EPS 13.59 元作為評價基礎，並給予歷史區間中緣偏上之
33 倍 PER，目標價設定為 450 元，給予買進之投資建議。

鴻海(2317 TT) Trading Buy

AI 平台世代交替，2027 展望樂觀，投資建議 Trading Buy。 
投資建議：展望 2027 年，Vera Rubin 將成為主要成長引擎，主
要客戶對於 AI 伺服器的需求維持強勁，因此我們給予鴻海
Trading Buy 的投資建議，目標價 272 元。 
3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元：3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，
QoQ+19%， YoY+46%，毛利率 6.02%， QoQ-0.1ppts，主要受產
品組合及原物料上漲影響導致毛利率下滑；營業利益 1,154.99
億元，QoQ+22%，營業利益成長高於營收及營業毛利，反映生
產進入規模經濟；歸屬母公司淨利 775.24 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51
元。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.7%，預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+11.7%：鴻海
10/2026 營收 1.31 兆元，MoM+12.7%， YoY+45.7%，創單月歷
史新高。展望 4Q26，我們預期伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費
電子方面將受惠新機推出與傳統旺季帶來的成長。群益預估
4Q26 營收 3.37 兆元，QoQ+11.7%；毛利率/營益率持續受 Vera 
Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，季減 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到
5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，QoQ+15.97%，稅
後 EPS 6.39 元。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：管理
層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin 出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027
年，整體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展
望維持正向，預估 AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。 
預估 2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，稅後 EPS 24.71 元：我們預估 2027
年營收 15.1 兆元，YoY+37.1%，毛利率 5.65%， YoY-0.4ppts； 
YoY-0.3ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 3,478.27 億元，YoY+25.4%，稅
後 EPS 24.71 元。Vera Rubin 由初期出貨轉為全年營收貢獻。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：   
環境：鴻海採用 TCFD 框架為基礎，全面盤點與評估氣候相關風險與機會，
並導入 ISO 14001 環境管理系統與 ISO 45001 職業安全衛生管理系統，同時
也推動「低碳供應鏈轉型推動計畫」，協助超過 30 家供應商完成節能專案，
累計減少碳排放約 15,000 公噸 CO₂e。鴻海已有超過 150 家供應商完成碳盤
查並簽署科學基礎減碳目標（SBTi），提升能源使用效率與減碳成效。  
社會：鴻海秉持「以人為本」理念，長期支持教育與青年培訓，設立「三創
育成基金會」，提供新創團隊資金與資源支持，自 2015 年以來已經培育超
過  100 組科技創業團隊，促進創新創業生態系發展，為社會培訓科技人才。
治理：董事會有設立「永續發展委員會」，主管由具備三年以上公開發行公
司財務主管經歷之財務長兼任，該委員自 2023 年起每年召開至少四次會議，
全體委員及相關主管亦於任職期間完成 ESG 專業訓練課程，強化永續治理
能力與資訊揭露透明度。 
公司簡介：  
鴻海精密工業股份有限公司（Hon Hai Precision Industry），成立於 1974 年，
由郭台銘創辦，總部位於新北市土城區。公司早期以模具及電子零組件製造
起家，逐步擴展至電子產品組裝、系統整合及設計製造服務，現為全球最大
電子專業製造服務（EMS）廠商。 
集團業務涵蓋消費智能產品、雲端網路產品、電腦終端產品及元件與其他產
品四大領域，旗下包含工業富聯（FII）、鴻騰精密（FIT）等重要子公司，
並透過全球事業單位及子公司分工，提供零組件、模組至整機系統的垂直整
合服務。集團生產及服務據點遍及全球逾 20 個國家及地區，包括中國、台
灣、印度、越南、墨西哥及美國等，全球員工人數於季節性高峰約達 100 萬
人。 
鴻海主要競爭對手包括廣達、緯創、和碩、仁寶及立訊精密等，依產品領域
有所不同。近年積極布局 AI 伺服器、電動車及半導體等新興業務，其中 AI
伺服器受惠全球雲端服務供應商（CSP）資本支出擴張，成為重要成長動能，
並持續鞏固全球電子製造服務龍頭地位。 
3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元： 3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，QoQ+19%，
YoY+46%，毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-0.1ppts，YoY-0.33ppts，主要受產品組合及
原物料上漲影響導致毛利率下滑；營業利益 1,154.99 億元，QoQ+22%，
YoY+64%，營益率 3.83%， QoQ+0.07ppts， YoY+0.4ppts，營業利益成長高
於營收及營業毛利，顯現隨營運規模擴大，生產進入規模經濟；歸屬母公司
淨利 775.24 億元，QoQ+29%， YoY+34%，稅後 EPS 5.51 元。營業利益、淨
利和 EPS 皆創下歷史單季新高。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.7%，預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+11.7%：鴻海 10/2026 營
收 1.31 兆元，MoM+12.7%， YoY+45.7%，創單月歷史新高，除受惠 GB300
持續放量外，iPhone 新機推出亦帶動營收顯著增長；Vera Rubin 僅小量出
貨。展望 4Q26，我們預期 GB200/300 出貨量季增持平至微幅下滑，Vera 
Rubin 則有雙位數以上的成長，並且由於 Vera Rubin 的 ASP 近乎倍增，預
估伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費電子方面，我們認為記憶體與處理器成本

3 群益投顧 
上升，將使手機出貨面臨壓力，但產品 ASP 提升及 Apple 穩健的品牌效應
仍將為出貨量提供支撐，因此 4Q26 仍將受惠新機推出與傳統旺季帶來的成
長。相較於消費電子的季節性，GB200/300 放量與產品組合變化對全年營運
的影響更大，因此 2026 年的主要成長引擎仍是伺服器業務的擴張；Vera 
Rubin 則為 2027 年的成長建立基礎。綜上所述，群益預估 4Q26 營收 3.37
兆元，QoQ+11.7%；毛利率/營益率持續受 Vera Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，
下滑 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到 5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，
QoQ+15.97%，稅後 EPS 6.39 元。 
雲端網路成為營運主軸，規模經濟支持本業獲利成長： 3Q26 雲端網路產品
占營收 54%，高於 2Q26 的 51%。消費智能、電腦終端及元件與其他產品分
別占 29%、12%及 5%。雲端網路產品受 AI 伺服器及機櫃出貨帶動，季增與
年增均強勁。消費智能產品亦受新品與傳統旺季帶動，季增優於公司原先預
期。據我們所知，伺服器約占雲端網路營收的 85%-90%，其中約 70%來自
AI 伺服器。隨雲端網路占比提高，AI 機櫃的交付時程、平台組合及物料認
列方式，將更直接影響鴻海的營收與獲利率。鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-
0.1ppts，主要受到整櫃 GPU 伺服器高昂的 BOM 所 稀 釋；營 業 利 益 率 3.83%，
QoQ+0.07ppts，雖然毛利率季減，營益率卻有所改善，反映出營收規模擴大
及營運效率提升的效益。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：AI 機櫃的新舊平
台交替是鴻海延續成長的核心動能。鴻海預計 2026 年 AI 伺服器機櫃出貨
較 2025 年倍增以上，且既有 GB 平台需求可能延續至 2027 年。此外，Vera 
Rubin 與 GB 系列同為 NVL72，因此我們預期鴻海將可較平滑的實現世代交
替。管理層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027 年，
伺服器業務上會新增更多 Neocloud 客戶，但主要客戶仍然是大型 CSP。整
體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展望維持正向，預估
AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。 
自製零組件與自動化支撐獲利，高 BOM 仍主導毛利率走勢：鴻海透過垂直
整合提高單一專案可承接的製造內容，管理層表示除規模經濟外，零組件自
製率的提高與自動化生產都將為毛利率及營益率提供支撐。根據我們調查，
在晶片以外的零組件自製率約 50%，布局包括連接器、線材、Midplane、交
換器及散熱相關產品，零組件毛利率高於 15%，部分甚至可達 20%，管理層
亦提出 800G 以上交換器全年營收倍增的展望，這些產品有助鴻海由機櫃組
裝向高速互聯及關鍵元件延伸，並可提升附加價值。然而據我們了解目前鴻
海在 GPU 的大型專案中尚未採用 consignment model，記憶體等元件漲價則
主要透過 cost-plus 模式轉嫁給客戶，進入 Vera Rubin 世代後，由於 ASP 將
近倍增，會進一步造成毛利率稀釋，考慮到 GPU Rack 單價極高，我們預估
2027 年毛利率仍將承壓。 
ASIC 拓展產品組合，物料模式與 ASP 差異使營收比重低於出貨量佔比：據
我們了解，鴻海 ASIC 平台約佔 AI 伺服器營收的 20%，較 1H26 略升，且
ASIC 平台成長速度高於 GPU 平台，除既有客戶外亦有新專案，管理層預估
3Q26 ASIC 伺服器出貨量 YoY+150%，高於 GPU 伺服器 70%的出貨量年增。
我們認為 ASIC 專案增加可擴大鴻海在不同運算平台的參與，減少成長來源
集中於單一 GPU 平台的程度。此外，大部分 ASIC 專案將有望採用
consignment model，有助於緩解 AI 伺服器對於整體毛利率的負面效果，然
而 GPU 平台 ASP 顯著高於 ASIC 平台，因此 ASIC 的營收較難完整反映其

4 群益投顧 
專案及製造規模。我們認為目前 ASIC 專案採用 consignment model 尚不足
以顯著改善 GPU Rack 高 BOM 對整體毛利率的稀釋。 
策略投資三菱電機，電動車可望成為未來另一成長引擎：鴻海透過與三菱電
機的策略結盟深化在電動車領域的發展，雖投資金額小於 AI 相關的支出，
但將持續投入研發，目前已做好充足準備量產，將尋機大規模進入 EV 市場，
可望成為未來的另一成長動能。 
預估 2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，稅後 EPS 24.71 元：我們預估隨 AI 機櫃放量
將提高營收與獲利規模，2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，YoY+37.1%，毛利率 5.65%，
YoY-0.4ppts；營業利益 5,181.73 億元，YoY+25.5%，營益率 3.43%，YoY-
0.3ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 3,478.27 億元，YoY+25.4%，稅後 EPS 24.71 元。
Vera Rubin 由初期出貨轉為全年營收貢獻，加上既有 GB300 需求延續及
ASIC 業務擴張，帶動全年營收顯著增長。公司持續布局台灣、美國、墨西
哥及越南產能，亦有助因應客戶在不同地區的交付需求。 
投資建議：受惠 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，2026 AI 機櫃出貨可望倍數增長。
此外我們預期憑藉 iPhone 的品牌效應，4Q26 智慧型消費電子業務可望維持
過往的傳統出貨旺季。展望 2027 年，Vera Rubin 將成為主要成長引擎，主
要客戶對於 AI 伺服器的需求依舊強勁。關鍵製程自動化及零組件自製率的
提升亦有助提升營運效率，為毛利率及營益率提供部分支撐。考量上述原
因，我們給予鴻海 Trading Buy 的投資建議，目標價 272 元。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

敬鵬 (2355 TT)

內外皆美

調升投資評等至買進，目標價 82 元，上漲空間 49%。 
1. 3Q26 毛利率突破 20%，EPS1.34 元，優於預期。 
2. 漲價及台幣貶值雙重有利因子加持，估計 4Q26 季增雙位數。 
3. 泰國新廠新增 68.23 億泰銖投資案。

3Q26 毛利率突破 20%，EPS1.34 元，優於預期 
3Q26 合併營收 48.12 億元，QOQ+13.5%，YOY+24.7%，，毛利率 20.5%，較前
一季 12.2%及同期 8.6%分別大增 8.3 及 11.9ppt，大幅優於市場共識，而毛利率提
升主要是順利反應原料成本及產品組合優化等，另匯兌利益挹注近一億元，單季稅
後淨利 5.33 億元，QOQ+196.6%、YOY+617.9%，EPS 1.34 元，表現優於預期。
累計前三季稅後淨利 9.6 億元，YOY+152.6%，EPS 2.42 元。

漲價及台幣貶值雙重有利因子加持，估計 4Q26 季增雙位數 
展望 4Q26，AI 產能供應不求外溢效益持續顯現，加上原料緊缺，下游車用客戶
漲價接受度提升，公司表示目前訂單需求遠大於供給，而營收及成長力道視
CCL、PP、鑽針..等原料供應狀況，現在主要瓶頸就是缺料。考量產品價格調漲及
近期台幣貶值有利因子，富邦估計季營收成長 16.5%，毛利率向上，單季
EPS1.73 元，QOQ+29.3%，YOY+171.2%。

泰國新廠新增 68.23 億元泰銖投資案 
泰國新廠預計 2026 年底前完工，預計 1H27 產出貢獻，公司表示該廠非單一客戶
專廠，產品應用也非單一。另因應未來營運發展需求，公司近日泰國廠董事會也通
過 68.23 億元泰銖投資案，預計 2026 年到 2029 年進行產能擴充的設備購置計劃。

富邦不同之處 
富邦為少數持續追蹤且出具更新報告券商之一。

評價與風險 
季報表現大幅優於市場預期，展望後市，受惠價格調漲順利及台幣貶值效益，營運
獲利向上，以 2027 年的 8.1 倍 P/E 評價來看並不高，因此我們調升投資評等至買
進，目標價 82 元，約相當 2027 年的 12 倍 P/E。

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition

Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating 
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

強茂(2481 TT) Buy

車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨帶動，投資建議 Buy。  
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資
料中心與 AI 伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 
Buy 的投資建議，目標價 160 元。 
車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨，3Q26 獲利三率三升：  
強茂 3Q26 營收 43.50 億元（QoQ +10.0%、 YoY +33.0%），主
因車用及 AI 需求帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合
優化提升至 37.3%（ QoQ +4.1 ppts、 YoY +6.0 ppts）；營業利益 
6.97 億元（QoQ +50.1%、 YoY +152.3%）；稅後淨利 6.49 億
元（QoQ +33.0%、 YoY +114.0%），3Q26 EPS 為 1.70 元。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高（YOY+2ppts），
車用產品營收較去年同期大幅成長 47.2% 。AI 與雲端運算營收
佔整體營收逾 12%，拉貨將逐步提升。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及
五座後段封測廠，隨著 2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加
入營運，進一步強化東南亞供應能力。預估 2026 年營收 
160.48 億元（YoY +22.6%），EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提
升：隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗提高，強茂成功切入美系 CSP 的  
ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈，主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）
與 ORing（冗餘電源管理兩大關鍵應用），推動 AI 佔比朝 15%
以上邁進。車用方面，除了 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放
量外，更推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日歐美市場。綜上所
述，預估強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元（YoY +19.8% ），EPS 
8.41 元。基於車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器新世代平台拉貨需求攀
升，給予 Buy 投資建議，目標價 160 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
在  ESG 永續發展表現上，強茂積極落實環境保護、供應鏈管理與綠色技術
創新三大面向： 
環境永續：持續投入節能減碳與能源轉型，透過製程節能改善及再生能源建
置，2025 年全年節電約 91.1 萬度，減少約 432 公噸 CO₂e 排放；太陽能系
統全年發電量突破 263 萬度，穩健提升綠電使用比重並朝向低碳營運邁進。
責任供應鏈：依循責任商業聯盟(RBA)標準深化供應鏈管理，年度完成 75 家
供應商 RBA 稽核且合格率達 100%，攜手合作夥伴打造具韌性的永續價值
鏈。 
創新與產品發展：持續深耕高效率功率半導體研發，2025 年集團累計有效
專利達 102 件，並維持 90 分以上的客戶滿意度，以高效能半導體解決方案
協助終端客戶提升能源使用效率，共同響應全球淨零碳排趨勢。

公司簡介： 
強茂成立於 1986 年，為台灣功率半導體領導廠商之一，主要從事離散元件
（ Discrete）之研發、製造與銷售，產品涵蓋 MOSFET 、Schottky、Rectifier、
TVS、 Zener、 Bridge Transistor、 ESD Protection 及  SiC 等。公司母公司專
注於核心元件開發，並透過關係企業與子公司進行垂直與水平整合，包含熒
茂光學、凡甲科技、虹冠電子、汎星半導體及新貿科技。強茂在低功率矽整
流器領域位居全球第四大供應商，並持續聚焦車用電子、雲端資料中心與 AI
伺服器等高附加價值應用。 
主要競爭對手分析 
1. 國際一線 IDM 大廠（高階車用與 AI 伺服器主要對手） 
強茂近年積極搶攻歐美車廠與 AI 機櫃市佔，主要在以下領域與國際巨頭
正面交鋒（並受惠於供應鏈去中化或對手產能受限之轉單效應）： 
• 安世半導體（Nexperia）：最直接的車用與小訊號元件競爭對手。安
世在車用小訊號二極體、ESD/TVS 保護元件及中低壓 MOSFET 市
佔率極高；每當安世出現產能吃緊或地緣政治/出口管制干擾時，歐美
Tier 1 車廠往往優先將訂單轉移至強茂。 
• 意法半導體（STMicroelectronics） 、 安森美（onsemi） 、 英飛凌
（ Infineon） ：全球車用與工業功率半導體前三大巨頭，在車規
MOSFET、SiC（碳化矽）及 AI 伺服器電源管理擁有完整生態系。強
茂以高性價比及交期彈性切入其二供（Second Source）體系。 
• 威世（Vishay）、達爾（Diodes Incorporated）、羅姆（ROHM）：
在整流二極體、蕭特基二極體及中低壓 MOSFET 產品線高度重疊，
是強茂在美系與日系客戶端的主要比價與份額競爭者。 
2. 台灣分離式元件同業 
• 台半（5425）：在整流二極體與車用 MOSFET 布局深厚（切入 Tier

### Page 3

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

3 群益投顧 
1 車廠供應鏈），與強茂在車用整流器與 40V/60V 車規 MOSFET 
領域重疊度最高。 
• 朋程（8255）：專注車用發電機二極體（全球市佔第一）及高效能二
極體（LLD/ULLD）、48V MOSFET 與  SiC 模組，在車用動力系統
與強茂具備相似的客戶群。 
• 富鼎（8261）、杰力（5299）、大中（6435）：台灣純 MOSFET 設
計公司（Fabless），主力集中於 PC/NB 、主機板與一般電源供應器，
與強茂在消費性電子及部分電源應用市場競爭。

圖一、強茂營收比重

資料來源：強茂，群益投顧彙整

車用市佔擴大與 AI 伺服器拉貨，3Q26 獲利三率三升： 
強茂 3Q26 營收 43.50 億元，QoQ+10.0%，YoY+33.0%，主因車用及 AI 需求

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

4 群益投顧 
帶動功率元件需求轉強；毛利率因產品組合優化提升至 37.3% ，
QoQ+4.1ppts，YoY+6.0ppts；營業利益 6.97 億元，QoQ+50.1%，YoY+152.3%，
呈現大幅提升；稅後淨利 6.49 億元，QoQ+32.96%， YoY+113.95%， 3Q26 
EPS 1.70 元，3Q26 財報表現亮眼。 
產品組合車用比重提升，產能佈局強化東南亞供應： 
YTD 3Q26 終端應用營收比重以車用 35%最高，年增 2 ppts，車用產品營收
較去年同期大幅成長 47.2%。 AI 與雲端運算營收佔整體營收逾 12%，受惠
新世代平台規格推進拉貨穩健。集團擁有三座晶圓廠及五座後段封測廠，隨
2026 年下半年越南胡志明市新封測廠加入營運，進一步強化東南亞第三供
應能力，2H26 營運持續增長，4Q26 營運預估與 3Q26 持平，預估 2026 年
營收 160.48 億元(YOY+22.56%)， EPS 為 5.47 元。 
車用市佔率持續擴大，MOSFET 業務加速放量與定價能力提升： 
強茂本身具有歐美車廠供應鏈能力，在安世半導體產能與出貨受阻後，客戶
端為確保供貨安全，加速將小訊號二極體與保護元件（ESD/TVS）訂單轉移
至強茂，使推升其車用營收比重與整體市佔率。隨著 AI 伺服器機櫃功耗
大幅提高，資料中心對供電穩定度與電源轉換效率的要求越發嚴苛。在成功
切入美系 CSP（雲端服務大廠）的 ASIC 伺服器與 AI 機櫃電源供應鏈後，
強茂主攻 Hot-Swap（熱插拔）與 ORing（冗餘電源管理）兩大關鍵應用。這
類高電流供電路徑需要具備「超低導通電阻（Low RDS(on) ）」的中高壓
MOSFET 來降低功耗與發熱，其 ASP 遠高於傳統消費性元件，預估 AI 佔比
可朝 15%以上邁進。 
車用展望方面，目前佔強茂整體營收比重約 35%。隨著電動車與智慧座艙滲
透率提升，車內馬達驅動、電池管理系統（BMS）及車載充電器對 MOSFET 
的用量呈倍數增長。強茂除了既有的 40V 車規中低壓 MOSFET 持續放量
外，更進一步推出碳化矽（SiC）MOSFET 搶攻日本與歐美車用市場，產品
線從小訊號保護元件成功延伸至高毛利的動力與電源核心。綜上所述，預估
強茂 2027 年營收 192.31 億元(YOY+19.8%)， EPS 8.41 元。 
投資建議：展望未來，隨著車用市佔率持續擴大，加上雲端資料中心與 AI 
伺服器新世代平台功率元件拉貨需求攀升，給予 Buy 的投資建議，目標價
160 元。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Global Research
First Read
Accton Technology Corporation
Q4 GM to see strong recovery
Q3 GM  down 2.1pp QoQ on higher component cost
Accton reported Q326 EPS of NT$23.39, below UBS/Consensus estimates on softer
margins. Q326 sales of NT$120.7bn (up 26% QoQ) was already posted, and slightly
below UBSe as its  AI card business (Trainium 3) shifted to consignment model for certain
higher cost components from mid-August. Q326 GM of 17.6% was down 2.1ppts QoQ

due to   higher component cost and ~1.5 months of  consignment, and we believe this
should be temporary as its customer will reimburse them in the subsequent quarters. Q3
OPEX increased by  15% QoQ, primarily due to higher R&D expenses, resulted in OPM of
13.4% (-1.6ppts QoQ). We estimate data center switch accounted for ~37% of Q326
sales (vs 36% in  Q226), while accelerator card was ~50% of total sales (50% in Q226).
Q4  growth on track despite full quarter of consignment

Accton expects Q426F revenue to grow high-single digit % QoQ with potential upside
for low-teens % QoQ growth, subject to component supply (i.e. switch IC) and
customers' year-end inventory adjustment, despite a full quarter of component
consignment (vs 1.5 month in Q3). It sees robust demand from  cloud switch  with the
social media customer revenue contribution exceeded e-commerce customer from
Q326, which is likely to sustain into Q426F. It also noted the social media customer has

secured more switch chipsets from Marvell and Nvidia Spectrum, and continues to
negotiate with Broadcom for better allocation. Accton also sees better unit and content
growth for Trainium 3 as the customer ramps liquid-cooled design, which carry ~20%
higher ASP and better GM due to increased design complexity. We forecast Q426E sales
growth of 9% QoQ, while its GM should recover to 19.9% (+2.3 ppt QoQ) on more
favourable mix and its customer reimbursement on the Q3 prepaid component costs.

Aggressive capacity expansion to support mid-term  growth
Accton announced  US$226 mn (~NT$7.3 bn) new capex on 12 Nov. for capacity
expansion in Taiwan and Vietnam, as well as construction of new fab/cleanroom in
Malaysia and Singapore. It aims to fund the capex through internal cashflow, bank
loans,  and has announced a 10mn shares of rights issuance (~2% dilution) on 22 Oct.
We believe this will drive its overall capacity to grow 40-50% YoY in 2027E, supporting

overall 2027E revenue to grow ~35% YoY, driven by 1) further ramp of Trainium 3 with
higher adoption of liquid cooling, 2) sustained 800G switch demand and migration to
1.6T with new customer addition, 3) increasing contribution from Optical Switches.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy with NT$3,200 PT
We  fine tune our model and raise   2026-28E EPS by 0-2%. We retain  Buy but lower  PT to
NT$3,200 (from   NT$3,688) based on 22x  average 2027-28E PE (from 26x) on overall

market valuation compression. We believe recent concerns over switch IC  shortages and
GM pressure from higher component costs are overdone as we expect Accton's GM to
recover from Q426E. Accton will host its annual investor conference in late November.

Company Description
Founded in 1988, Accton specialises in ODM/OEM solutions for data center switches, AI
accelerator cards, enterprise/ODM switches, carrier access/telecom and others. Accton is one
of the largest vendors of white-box and OCP-compliant switches globally (by sales).

Leveraging deep expertise in high-speed switching, system integration and rapid time-to-
market, it has secured long-term partnerships with major cloud service providers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Accton using PE methodology.
Key risks include: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for data center switches; 2) slower ramp
of 800G and 1.6T data center switches; 3) market share loss in the AI accelerator card
business; and 4) softer gross margin than expected.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

研究員：陳俐妍 ly.c@capital.com.tw

公司基本資訊 
目前股本(百萬元) 863
市值( 億元) 3,172
目前每股淨值( 元 ) 738.49
外資持股比(%) 50.13
投信持股比(%) 6.03
董監持股比(%) 2.27
融資餘額(張 ) 4,495
現金股息配發率(%) 47.08

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

世芯-KY(3661 TT) Buy

新客戶加入，挹注 2027 年營運成長，建議 Buy。  
投資建議：世芯-KY 受惠北美 CSP 客戶 N3 AI 加速器量產，
2026 年營運大幅成長；展望 2027 年，除既有 N3 專案持續放量
外，美系新客戶約 10 億美元專案預計自 2Q27 起貢獻量產營收，
將推動營收及獲利進一步成長。中長期 N2 AI 加速器亦持續推
進，預計 2028 年進入量產，成為下一階段成長動能，預估 2026
年及 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 113.66 元及 193.02 元。惟隨量產
(COT)業務占比提高，營收成長未必同步帶動毛利率提升，且
CSP 持續提升自主設計能力，將是影響世芯-KY 長期商業模式
及評價的重要風險。我們認為上述風險主要影響中長期估值上
限，但未來 12 個月仍受惠 N3 放量及新客戶加入，基本面成長
趨勢明確。因此建議 Buy，目標價 4,635 元 (PER24X)。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元：北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片已
正式啟動量產出貨，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億
元，QoQ+240.99%。晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營
收產生約 1 億美元之負面影響。受量產業務營收佔比拉升影響，
3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個百
分點。3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
取得美系新客戶訂單: 世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽
訂重大合約並展開執行，該案量約 10 億美元，合約已正式生效
並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季增加，該專案確
定應用於 AI 領域。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對
象，非中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出
規模，研判最具可能性之潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之
北美雲端巨頭；世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切
入合作，有效緩解客戶端工程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指
出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全新商業模式之開端，
該新客戶將躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。

2 群益投顧 
  世芯-KY ESG：  
世芯電子身為全球 IC 設計服務領導廠商，為確保所有產品皆為符合環境安
全衛生規定之綠色產品，對供應鏈管理不遺餘力，與供應商合作將綠色環保
的政策及概念推廣到整個供應，並一起朝向減少地球環境之衝擊共同努力，
其中主要包括： 
有害物質管制 
符合世芯電子相關規範及國內、外法令等國際環境規範 
滿足世芯電子新外包商評鑑程序 
滿足世芯電子外包商管理程序 
滿足世芯電子環境管理物質管理程序 
歐盟危害物質限用指令 (EU RoHS Compliance) 
歐盟新化學品政策 (REACH) 
無鹵材料 (Halogen Free) 
綠色物料之承認管理 
供應商提出第三方對產品生產材料所作的環境物質分析報告或證明文件。 
綠色資訊平台之建置 
建構系統化之 IT 資料庫輔助管理系統，藉此平台，便利上下游廠商綠色零
件之交換，並帶動上游廠商綠色零件供應的能力，降低環保要求的衝擊程
度。 
建立環境管理系統 - 供應商必須擁有 ISO14001 環境管理系統或其他相關
環境管理系統相關驗證。 
無衝突礦產保證書-(Declaration of Metal Conflict-Free) 
衝突金屬係指來自剛果民主共和國衝突礦區之礦物，類別有鈮鉭鐵礦，錫
石，黑鎢礦與黃金等。這些礦物提煉成鉭(Ta)、錫(Sn)、鎢(W) 、鈷(Co) 和
金 (Au) 等，分別用於電子和其他產品。世芯電子強烈要求供應商提供的原
物料中不得間接或直接使用衝突礦產。 
企業社會責任 (CSR) - 確保供應商落實企業社會責任。 
業務連續性計劃 (BCP) 
供應商須有一套基於業務運行規律的流程，使組織在突發事件面前能夠迅
速作出反應，以確保關鍵業務功能可以持續，而不造成業務中斷或業務流程
本質的改變。 
綠色管理稽核 - 世芯-KY 每年持續對供應商進行稽核、改善及教育訓練，
經由系統化運作與管理，確保提供品質精良之卓越產品給客戶與消費者。

3 群益投顧 
N3 貢獻增加，3Q26 營收 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%，稅後淨利 32.51 億
元，QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元： 
北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片 已於 05/2026 正式啟動量產出貨，之後營
收逐月向上，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%。
在產能限制方面，晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營收產生約 1 億
美元之負面影響，若無此類供應限制，單季營收原可增加 1 億美元。資料中
心以外之其他應用在 3Q26 合計創造超過 1 億美元營收，其中中國車用 ADAS
需求維持強勁，客戶次世代晶片如期於 4Q26 進行流片(Tape-out)。  
3Q26 各應用佔公司營收比重，HPC 佔公司營收比重 87%，佔比仍為最高，
相較 2Q26 的 50%與 1Q26 的 70%大幅提升。消費性電子(Consumer)佔比降
至 8%，低於 2Q26 的 16%與 1Q26 的 17%。 Networking 佔 5%，較 2Q26 的
32%與 1Q26 的 10%下降。利基型(Niche，含超級電腦、虛擬貨幣、柏青哥
機台、醫療設備與監視系統)佔 0%，低於 2Q26 的 1%與 1Q26 的 3%。  
製程營收比重，N3/N2 佔公司營收比重大幅躍升至 80%，較 2Q26 的 47%與
1Q26 的 21%大幅增加，主因北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器持續放量出貨。
7nm/5nm 佔比降至 15%，較 2Q26 的 40%下滑。16/12nm 佔比為 3%，較 2Q26
的 6%下降。28nm 及以下製程佔比為 1%，低於 2Q26 的 7%。  
地區營收分布隨著 N3 加速器放量出貨，地區分布再度出現結構性轉變：北
美地區佔比穩居第一大市場，達 84%(2Q26 為 51%， 1Q26 為 23%)。亞太地
區佔比降至 12%(2Q26 為 25%， 1Q26 為 47%)，車用晶片出貨維持穩健。其
他地區佔 4%(2Q26 為 17%， 1Q26 為 22%)。日本地區佔 1%(2Q26 為 7%，
1Q26 為 8%)。   
毛利率方面，3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個
百分點，為產品組合的最差情境，主因量產營收比重大幅拉升，致使委託設
計 (NRE)營收貢獻比例低於預期，加上數個專案之 NRE 認列產生遞延所致。
不過營業利益與稅後淨利同步創高。3Q26 世芯-KY 稅後淨利 32.51 億元，
QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
N2 專案將在 2026 年底 Tape-Out：  
世芯-KY 承接之美系 CSP 次世代 N2 AI 加速器專案，將採用異質小晶片
(Heterogeneous Chiplet) 架構。晶片總面積維持在約 4 個光罩尺寸，但相較
於 N3 世代，N2 專案之運算晶粒(Compute Die) 配置、內部互連架構
(Interconnect)及封裝型式(2.5D/3D)均出現顯著改動。由於小晶片設計涉及
多個獨立晶( 包括主運算晶粒與 I/O 晶粒) ，各晶粒之開發週轉時間
(Turnaround Time)不同，專案將採取分階段進行多次定案(Tape-out)之模式。
2026 年底 Tape-out ， 2027 年底放量 
次世代 N2 加速器已進入最終實作階段，預計自 4Q26 開始展開流片，目標
於 4Q27 達成小量試產(Low volume production) ，並於 2028 年迎來量產放
量，推動世芯-KY 自 2028 年初邁入全新成長階段。N2 與 N3 在晶片設計複
雜度上有顯著差異，N3 主要為 SoC 設計結合 2.5D 封裝，而 N2 雖維持 2.5D
封裝架構，但已全面轉向 Chiplet 架構搭配全環繞閘極(GAA)製程技術。世
芯 -KY 負責 N2 晶片各個部分之實體設計並進行整合，其在 N2 專案之設計
參與深度與整體專案規模均顯著大於 N3。專案不依賴特定同業之第三方 IP，

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
均採用通用型解決方案。在商業與生產模式上，世芯-KY 為 N2 之唯一實體
設計服務商，而在量產階段，客戶目前承諾僅會有客戶本身與世芯-KY 兩方
直接向台積電下單投片，世芯-KY 在 N2 之供應鏈地位與競爭優勢將較 N3
更為穩固。關於小晶片整合，包含 I/O Die 在內之晶片均由世芯-KY 統籌實
體設計，未來若因應 UALink 或 NVLink Fusion 等技術規格變更推出其他版
本之 I/O Die，世芯-KY 亦具備承接物理設計之先機。 
COT 合作模式短中期是有利世芯-KY，但長期要觀察 CSP 廠的全面自主設
計的進度： 
各大 CSP 為降低對傳統純 ASIC 廠商(如 Broadcom)高定價的依賴並實現
Cost-down，正全面轉向 COT 模式(客戶負責前端設計，世芯負責實體設計
與後端整合)。專案朝向「運算晶粒(Compute die)與  I/O 晶粒分工設計」發
展，世芯已與潛在客戶洽談負責運算晶粒並與外部 I/O 晶片整合的模式。
CSP 客戶轉向 COT 模式，本質上源於 3 項戰略考量： 
避免供應商鎖定(Vendor Lock-in) ：多數競爭對手採用垂直整合的封閉模式
(Captive Model)，從 IP、設計服務到封裝一條龍自主掌控，客戶一旦導入即
難以切換。CSP 客戶對此高度警惕，傾向採用第三方開放解決方案以保有供
應商多元化的彈性，降低單一供應商造成的議價劣勢與供應鏈風險。 
成本透明度與預算自主性：COT 模式下，客戶對晶圓、IP、設計服務及其他
供應鏈成本具有較高的掌握度，實際報酬及成本分攤方式則依個別專案合
約而定。相較於封閉模式下由供應商主導整體產品定價，COT 模式有助於
客戶提高成本控管能力與預算可預測性。 
規模擴張下的成本節省潛力： AI 晶片專案的出貨量與單位價值均遠高於傳
統晶片，隨著量產規模持續放大，COT 模式下透明且固定的利潤結構所帶
來的成本節省效益將等比例放大，對 CSP 客戶的財務影響不容忽視。 
然而，COT 模式對世芯-KY 的財務表現構成若干限制： 
COT 模式下的獲利空間取決於合約條件與服務範圍：COT 模式下，客戶通
常對專案成本及供應鏈有較高的掌握度，世芯-KY 的獲利則取決於其承接
的設計服務範圍、專案管理責任及個別合約的報酬安排。相較於自行掌握完
整產品定價與供應鏈的模式，若專案採成本加成或事先約定服務報酬的方
式，世芯-KY 透過晶片價格上漲或供應鏈成本變動擴大利潤的空間可能較
為有限。因此，未來毛利率及獲利表現，仍須觀察各專案的服務內容、合約
條件及營收組合變化。 
多方協作帶來的執行複雜度與供應鏈風險： COT 模式涉及晶圓廠、IP 供應
商、基板廠、封裝廠及測試廠等多個外部夥伴的協同整合，任一環節的產能
短缺或交期延誤均可能波及整體專案進度。世芯-KY 在 N7 世代曾因 ABF 基
板短缺而承受供應鏈壓力，此一歷史案例揭示開放生態系模式下供應鏈管
理的內在脆弱性。儘管公司已大幅增加供應商來源以分散風險，但多源管理
本身亦帶來額外的協調成本與品質控管挑戰。 
IP 自主能力缺失，技術話語權受限：世芯-KY 目前選擇與 Synopsys 等第三
方 IP 供應商合作，而非自行開發 IP。此策略雖降低研發資本支出，卻也意
味著公司在核心技術環節缺乏自主掌控能力，在特定 IP 出現供應限制或授
權條件變動時，可能面臨被動局面，長期而言亦限制公司在技術差異化上的
發展空間。

### Page 5

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

5 群益投顧 
規模成長不必然帶動利潤率同步提升：在固定利潤百分比的框架下，營收規
模的擴大主要反映的是晶圓與供應鏈成本的等比例放大，而非公司自身附
加價值的提升。換言之，世芯-KY 的獲利成長高度依賴出貨量的持續擴張，
而非利潤率的結構性改善，此一特性使公司的盈利模式更接近於量能驅動
型 (Volume-driven)，而非價值驅動型(Value-driven)。  
整體而言，世芯-KY 等 IC 設計服務公司的商業模式恰好能針對不同客戶的
能力缺口提供精準介入。無論客戶需求是後端實體設計落地、部分實體設計
協作、I/O Chiplet 的 IP 整合，乃至純量產委外，世芯均具備對應的服務能
量。此種高度彈性的模組化接案模式，使公司得以滲透不同成熟度的客戶群
體，有效降低拿單門檻。 
總而言之，CSP 晶片自研能力的持續演進是評估世芯-KY 長期投資價值不
可迴避的核心風險。從演進路徑來看，CSP 通常由依賴完整 ASIC 服務起步，
逐步過渡至主導架構設計但委外實體落地，最終邁向全面設計自主。世芯-
KY 目前所享有的設計服務需求，本質上是 CSP 處於此過渡階段所產生的結
構性紅利，一旦 CSP 設計能力全面成熟，世芯-KY 的角色將面臨從「策略
性設計夥伴」退化為「可替代供應鏈中介」的根本性風險，商業模式的可持
續性將受到挑戰。短期而言，N3 乃至 N2 先進製程實體設計的高度複雜性，
以及多代合作累積的工程默契，仍構成一定的切換成本緩衝。然而隨著 EDA
工具智能化持續壓低設計門檻，此一護城河的侵蝕速度是要警惕。群益認
為，世芯-KY 能否在當前紅利窗口期內成功深化 IP 能力、拓展先進封裝技
術佈局並實現客戶多元化，將是決定其長期商業模式能否升級躍遷的關鍵
觀察指標。 
N3 專案為 2026 年和 2027 年主要的營收貢獻： 
世芯-KY 目前有超過 60 項 FinFET 專案和 20 個以上 CoWoS 案子，平均每
年 20~30 new tape-out，且獲得多個 N3 專案，也有 N2 專案進行中。其中最
重要的專案是為北美 CSP 開發的 N3 AI 加速器(Trainium 3)。該專案在 4Q25
啟動，2Q26 末已進入量產。N5 完整的專案營收貢獻約 35 億美元，N3 的營
收貢獻會高很多，除主要客戶專案之外，世芯-KY 還贏得來自北美客戶的多
個 N3 專案，包括網路客戶以及北美和歐洲的主要網路設備廠商。 
現行 N3 晶片已符合各項測試標準且良率維持在合理水準，目前正全速出
貨，為 2026 年最大營收貢獻專案，亦將維持為 2027 年最大營收來源。管理
層預期當前世代專案將量產約 2 年時間，隨後由次世代接替。該專案預期於
4Q26 營收呈現小幅季增，並於 2027 年呈現大幅增長。世芯-KY 過去因營運
資金有限而在該專案初期未深化溝通，但後續成功自競爭對手處贏回此專
案，目前主要營運與產能限制已獲得解決，已做好承接更大出貨規模之準
備。 
取得美系新客戶訂單，預期於 2027 年躍升為世芯-KY 第二大客戶： 
世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽訂重大合約並展開執行，該專案整
體生命週期累計營收規模約 10 億美元，量產營收預計自 2Q27 開始貢獻，
後續逐季增加。已正式生效並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季
維持季增趨勢，該專案確定應用於 AI 領域，推估採用台積電 N3 先進製程，
專案資產由雙方共同擁有。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對象，非
中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出規模，市場推測可能

                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

6 群益投顧 
為潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之北美雲端巨頭 Meta 或 Microsoft；
世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切入合作，有效緩解客戶端工
程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全
新商業模式之開端，具備良好獲利能力且長期可持續，該新客戶自 2Q27 起
之量產放量將推動其躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。 
2027 年 N3 案子貢獻持續，及美系新客戶加入，預估 2026 年和 2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 113.66 元和 193.02 元： 
世芯-KY 2026 年 ~2028 年營收將會再大幅成長，因為 1)北美大客戶新世代
產品 N3 量產和下一代設計合作，公司預計其關鍵客戶在 2028 年前將有強
勁且可持續的營收貢獻；營收從 2Q26 開始顯著躍升。2)美系大型巨頭新客
戶的 10 億美元，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季季增。3)車用業務
ADAS 方面，預計產品將成為從 2026 年開始的主要營收貢獻者之一。下一
代產品的開發已在進行中，確保營收連續性並為業務帶來強勁動能，直到
2028 年。 
受惠於 N3 AI加速器量產出貨小幅季增，疊加次世代 N2 專案展開流片(Tape-
out)與大型設計案啟動，4Q26 營收預估將達到 8.5~9 億美元區間，改寫單季
歷史新高。由於量產營收比重仍高達 80%以上，4Q26 毛利率預估將維持在
18~19.5%區間，公司預期 2026 年全年毛利率將達 20%以上。預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS 113.66 元。 
邁入 2027 年，1Q27 營收預估小幅拉回，自 2Q27 起，隨著規模達 10 億美
元的美國新大客戶量產營收正式啟動，營運將重啟逐季強勁增長動能，單季
營收將突破 10 億美元大關。3Q27 至 4Q27 在現有 N3 專案擴大出貨以及次
世代 N2 加速器於 4Q27 展開小量產(Low volume production) 挹注下，預估
2027 年稅後 EPS 193.02 元。 
2028 年次世代 N2 加速器進入量產： 
展望 2028 年，世芯-KY 大客戶的次世代 N2 加速器進入大規模量產階段，
由於 N2 專案轉向全環繞閘極(GAA)與 Chiplet 先進架構，世芯-KY 表示將
負責全晶片實體設計與整合，晶片價值量與專案規模將顯著超越 N3 世代。
因此管理層評估 2028 年營收具備再次翻倍之潛力，但群益先以保守情境預
估。 
在營運擴張過程中，世芯-KY 面臨兩大核心營運限制，分別為營運資金
(Working Capital)承載力以及高階研發人力瓶頸。在資金層面，管理層明確
指出 2028 年營收成長的瓶頸已非市場需求，而是資產負債表的承載能力。
由於雲端服務商(CSP)先進製程晶圓與先進封裝代工代採購金額極為龐大，
支撐 40 億美元以上營收需要巨額營運資金墊付晶圓廠貨款，公司資產負債
表結構將直接決定能承接的量產規模上限。在人力層面，單一先進製程大型
專案往往需要高達 150 位工程師投入，在 N3 出貨、N2 實作與新客戶專案
同步推進下，研發資源短缺成為主要限制，迫使公司採取高度篩選專案之策
略，僅承接具備實質商業營收之大型案件。為突破研發人力天花板，世芯-
KY 目前正積極對外擴編，預計 2026 年底員工人數將突破千人規模，並在
前端實作與驗證導入專屬內部 AI 訓練模型以大幅節省中段研發人力，但在
最終實體設計階段仍高度仰賴頂尖工程人力，高階人才擴充速度將是公司
長期承接新專案的關鍵決定因素。

10 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3Q26 Review: 악재의 중첩, 
4분기까지 지켜보자 
최정환 CFA

jeonghwan.choi@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 1,640,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
739,000 
방위산업

◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
◼ 컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
◼ 4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 1,640,000원 유지 
4Q26E-2Q27E 지상방산 및 항공우주 EBITDA에 Target 12MF EV/EBITDA 25.0x 
적용, 상장 자회사 가치 SOTP 통해 목표주가 산출. 3Q26 실적 아쉬우나 투자포
인트에는 변함 없어 멀티플은 변경 없이 적용. 27년 실적 추정치 상향에 따라 본
업 가치 상승, 26년 스페인 자주포 양산 및 미국 자주포 시제 사업자로 선정되며 
현지화 전략이 유효했음을 증명. 3Q26 실적에 대한 우려를 소화하며 글로벌 피어
들과의 멀티플 격차 축소를 전망함

컨센서스 및 추정치 대비 매출, 영업이익 하회 
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 6.1조원(-6.0% YoY), 영업이익 4,520억원(-46.2% YoY, 
OPM 7.4%)으로 컨센서스인 매출액 7.4조원 및 영업이익 1.1조원을 크게 하회
하는 실적 시현. 하회의 주요 요인으로는 3Q26 필리조선소 적자폭 확대, 지상
방산 부문 실적이 부진한 영향. 지상방산 부문의 경우 매출액 1.2조원(-41.0% 
YoY), 영업이익 220억원(-96.2% YoY, OPM 1.7%)을 시현했는데, 폴란드 관련 
매출이 부재했던 1Q26 영업이익률 16.9%보다도 낮은 실적을 보임. 이는 국내
외 사업 모두에서 악재가 겹쳐 발생했던 것으로 보이는데 국내 사업에서는 마
진율이 낮은 사업이 매출로 인식되었고 수출 사업에서는 폴란드 매출 부재 뿐 
아니라 이집트, 호주 K9 자주포 매출 인식 또한 예상 대비 부진해 고정비 레버
리지 효과로 3Q26 영업이익률이 크게 훼손된 것으로 추정. 이외 대전사업장 복
구에 따른 비용, 기타 사업 충당금 등 총 500억원 수준의 일회성 비용이 발생했
으며 영업외손익에서는 원화 강세에 따른 환손실이 주로 반영되었음

4Q26 및 27년 실적 추정치 상향 
1Q26에서 3Q26까지 이뤄지지 않았던 폴란드 EC2 K9 자주포 30문 인도는 
4Q26에 집중될 것으로 보이며 폴란드 EC1 천무 또한 기존 가이던스였던 26년 
천무 40대에서 60대로 인도 대수 상향이 이뤄짐. 이에 따라 4Q26 지상방산 영
업이익률은 22.8%로 회복을 전망. 27년에는 폴란드 K9 자주포 및 천무 인도 대
수 증가로 26년 수출 비중 58.0%에서 77.2%로 증가, 이에 따라 영업이익률 또
한 매출 믹스 개선에 따라 19.8%에서 28.0%로 개선을 전망

기업 투자의견 
- Buy(매수)  
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 상승 예상 
- Marketperform(시장수익률) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 -10%p~10%p 주가 변동 예상 
- Underperform(시장수익률 하회) 
  : 향후 6개월간 시장수익률 대비 10%p 이상 주가 하락 예상

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20 개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500 억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

Document 339: European Economics Analyst_ UK Outlook 2027_ Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals

Source PDF: all report pdf/European Economics Analyst_ UK Outlook 2027_ Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-15
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

The UK economy proved surprisingly resilient in 2026. Growth accelerated n
despite rising energy prices and tighter financial conditions, and the labour 
market showed signs of stabilisation. The new government navigated a difficult 
fiscal backdrop to deliver a low-key budget that maintained the consolidation 
path. 
The coming quarters are likely to be more challenging. First, we expect growth to n
slow as the impact of the energy shock on consumer spending increases over the 
winter. Second, inflation will rise further as electricity and gas bills increase and 
the indirect effects of the energy shock build. We expect this to push the BoE to 
hike a second time, probably in February. Third, the government faces a difficult 
Spending Review; existing spending plans look too tight, but the Chancellor has 
limited fiscal headroom. 
Nonetheless, we see compelling reasons to be constructive on the economic n
outlook for 2027. First, some of the recent growth improvement looks structural, 
and the productivity trend is strengthening. We therefore expect activity to 
reaccelerate later in the year as some of the cyclical headwinds fade, and our 
Q4/Q4 growth forecast is above consensus at 1.4%. Second, we do not expect 
strong second-round effects from the energy shock into domestic inflation, so 
the BoE is likely to hike significantly less than priced. Third, we expect further 
fiscal consolidation, and a more favourable OBR forecast could ease the 
trade-offs facing the Chancellor. 
We also see scope for sentiment around the UK’s longer-term economic n
prospects to improve. The UK is well-placed to benefit from AI adoption given its 
strengths in professional services, and surveys indicate that companies expect a 
material boost to productivity growth in the coming years. The PM has also 
recently raised the possibility of closer EU ties, which could bring notable 
economic gains over time.

### Page 2

UK Outlook 2027: Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals

The UK economy proved surprisingly resilient in 2026. Despite the energy shock keeping 
inflation elevated and causing the BoE to pivot from cutting to hiking, growth 
accelerated this year. The labour market showed signs of improvement, with 
unemployment falling and employment growth indicators ticking up. The new 
government navigated a challenging fiscal backdrop to deliver a low-key Budget that 
maintained the consolidation path.

Cyclical Headwinds 
The coming quarters are likely to prove more challenging in several respects. First, we 
expect growth to slow in the near term and run slightly below trend in Q4 and the first 
half of 2027. The impact of the energy shock on household finances is likely to build over 
the winter, with weak real disposable income growth resulting in a slowdown in 
consumer spending.

Exhibit 1: Growth Proved Surprisingly Resilient in 2026

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   2
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 3

Tighter financial conditions should also weigh on growth as mortgages are refixed at 
higher rates. Fiscal policy will likely turn more contractionary compared with this year 
given that current plans imply a larger consolidation in the primary balance in 2027.

While the labour market data improved over the last few months, we expect slower 
near-term growth to result in a modest further weakening. That is consistent with 
continued softness in most indicators of employment intentions. The pace of loosening 
should be gradual as the peak impact of last year’s labour cost increases on employment 
growth has probably passed. But we see the unemployment rate rising back to 5% by the 
middle of next year.

Exhibit 2: Consumer Spending Growth Likely to Slow

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics

Exhibit 3: Growth Headwinds from Rising Mortgage Rates and Tighter Fiscal Policy

Note that student loan figures for 2026-27 are affected by the accounting impact of a freeze in repayment thresholds, so the figures excluding student loans may 
give a better guide to the impacts on demand.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg, OBR
15 November 2026   3
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 4

Second, inflation is likely to increase further in the coming months. Consumer energy 
prices will rise notably in January on the back of higher electricity and gas bills. We also 
expect food and core goods inflation to accelerate as the indirect effects of the energy 
shock become more apparent. We also see renewed upward pressure in some regulated 
services prices that are indexed to headline inflation, and higher fuel costs will likely raise 
transport services prices. Overall, we expect inflation to peak at 4.3% in February and 
average 4.1% in 2027H1.

The peak in headline inflation will likely exceed the threshold at which the BoE has 
typically become more concerned about second-round effects. Taken together with 
hawkish recent commentary, that points to another 25bp BoE hike to 4.25%. This will 
probably come in February, although an earlier December move is also possible.

Exhibit 4: Renewed Labour Market Softness

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England

Exhibit 5: Rising Inflationary Pressures Point to a Second BoE Hike

The forecasts shown on the left are conditional on GS forecasts for energy prices.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   4
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 5

Third, the government faces a difficult Spending Review. Existing plans imply a notable 
slowdown in departmental spending growth from next year onwards, which looks 
challenging to deliver given pressures on the health and defence budgets. History 
suggests that these plans will probably be notably upgraded. New policy priorities could 
require further outlays, with PM Burnham’s agenda likely to become clearer after the 
publication of his 10-Year Plan. But financing higher spending may be challenging given 
limited fiscal headroom, with revenues constrained by Labour’s manifesto not to raise 
taxes on working people. Welfare reforms could bring some savings over time but look 
likely to be accompanied by upfront investment in employment support.

Structural Improvement 
Despite these cyclical challenges, we see several reasons to be more constructive on the 
UK’s economic prospects for 2027. First, some of the tailwinds that supported growth 
this year look structural. Strength in business investment has partly reflected rising 
information and communications capex, which looks likely to be sustained by ongoing 
data centre investment. This investment should support continued growth in technology 
services output, where demand has increased on the back of AI adoption. Further 
growth in utilities capital spending linked to renewables should also continue to support 
demand.

Exhibit 6: A Challenging Spending Review

Source: OBR
15 November 2026   5
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 6

The productivity trend also appears to be improving, as growth in output per worker has 
increased significantly recently. Some of this may reflect the impact of last year’s 
employer National Insurance hike on hiring, but more recent data also show gains in 
sectors such as professional services that were less affected by labour cost increases. We 
also see more fundamental reasons to expect a pickup. While Brexit has probably 
weighed significantly on growth over the past decade, the worst of the headwind has 
likely passed. Survey data from the Decision Maker Panel indicate that early gains from 
AI adoption could also have modestly boosted the figures.

These factors, together with declines in energy prices, should mean that growth 
reaccelerates through the second half of next year. Overall, we expect a 1.4% Q4/Q4 
increase in GDP, above consensus expectations as well as the BoE and OBR forecasts.

Exhibit 7: Some Growth Drivers Look More Structural

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Yotzov et al. (2026)

Exhibit 8: Trend Productivity Growth is Probably Improving

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Various Academic Studies
15 November 2026   6
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 7

Second, we continue to expect limited second-round effects into domestic price 
pressures. Even after the recent decline in the unemployment rate, broader measures of 
utilisation (such as the vacancy/unemployment ratio) suggest that the labour market 
remains far looser than in 2022/23. That will likely reduce the extent to which higher 
headline inflation passes through into wage pressures. Indeed, early data indicate that 
pay settlements are likely to remain stable next year, and the BoE’s Decision Maker Panel 
survey shows little change in wage expectations since the start of the Middle East 
conflict. Private sector regular pay growth will probably accelerate from low levels but is 
likely to remain well below the levels seen after the previous energy shock.

With only a modest increase in pay growth, we expect overall labour cost inflation to be 
well below the levels seen in recent years. This is especially true in consumer-facing 
sectors, which saw sharper increases in wages over recent years and the largest impact 
from the employer National Insurance increases. More moderate labour cost inflation

Exhibit 9: Our GDP Forecast is Slightly Above Consensus

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg, Bank of England, OBR

Exhibit 10: Second-Round Effects Likely to be Moderate

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   7
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 8

should help to keep a lid on underlying services inflation, despite cost pressures from 
higher energy and food prices.

Given moderate second-round effects, we think that the BoE will hike less than priced. 
After tightening policy in February, we expect the MPC to keep Bank Rate at 4.25% for 
the rest of the year, whereas the market implies two further rate increases. The risks 
around our baseline are skewed towards further tightening, but even on a 
probability-weighted basis our forecast is notably below current market pricing.

Third, 2027 is likely to see renewed progress on deficit reduction. The current fiscal 
plans imply a steep reduction in the primary deficit next year. While we think spending 
could overshoot the official projections, tax receipts could also come in somewhat 
higher. Further consolidation should mean that the UK continues to compare favourably 
to other advanced economies in terms of reducing borrowing. While the Spending 
Review does pose a challenge for the government, it is possible that changes in the

Exhibit 11: More Limited Labour Cost Increases Should Keep a Lid on Underlying Services Inflation

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, ONS

Exhibit 12: We Expect the BoE to Hike Less Than Priced

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Bloomberg
15 November 2026   8
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 9

economic forecast could help the Chancellor. The OBR’s wage growth forecast continues 
to look low, and an upgrade would boost the tax revenues projection, increasing fiscal 
space.

A Positive Long-Run Outlook 
We also see scope for sentiment around the UK’s longer-term economic prospects to 
improve. The UK is well-placed to benefit from AI adoption given its specialisation in 
high-value added services. IMF estimates indicate that a relatively high share of the 
workforce is exposed to AI, with a particularly large number of workers in roles that may 
see productivity gains but are less likely to be displaced. Survey data suggest that firms 
expect adoption to result in sizeable productivity increases in the coming years. Even 
though these gains are unlikely to be fully additive to the current productivity trend, the 
numbers still indicate upside risks to our estimates for supply growth.

Exhibit 13: Continued Fiscal Consolidation

On the left, the UK data are shown on a public sector basis.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, OBR, Bank of England
15 November 2026   9
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 10

The UK is also well-positioned in several high growth innovative industries. This includes 
a world-leading position in life sciences, such as pharmaceuticals, biotechnology, 
medical technology, and genomics. The UK has a world-class research base, with the 
London/Oxford/Cambridge triangle a leading biotech cluster. 
Another possible source of long-run growth upside would be closer ties with the EU. The 
upcoming (November 20) summit could see some progress, with the two sides expected 
to finalise agrifood and emissions trading deals. But the Prime Minister has also 
indicated that he will use the summit to outline options for how the relationship might 
develop after the next election, potentially including a customs union, rejoining the 
single market, or even a second referendum on membership. Any reset would require 
negotiation, political trade-offs, and time before benefits materialise. But it could bring 
significant economic upside, so signals towards improved trading links could start to lift 
optimism on the UK’s growth prospects.

Exhibit 14: Well-Placed to Benefit from AI Adoption

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, IMF, Bank of England

Exhibit 15: Signals Towards Closer EU Ties Could Boost Sentiment on Long-Run Growth

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Various Academic Studies, Dhingra and Sampson (2019)

（前期比、％）
（年・四半期）
実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値

１. 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナ
ス成長となった（当研究所予測 10月 30日：前期比▲0.1％、年率▲0.3％）。 
２. 物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で民間消費が前期比
▲0.3％の減少となったことがマイナス成長の主因である。一方、高水準の企業収益を背
景に設備投資は前期比 1.6％の高い伸びとなった。 
３. 外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。
財貨・サービスの輸出が前期比▲0.2％と減少する一方、代替調達の進展に伴う原油輸入
量の急増を主因として財貨・サービスの輸入が同 2.5％の増加となった。 
４. 原油の代替調達に伴う輸入増加による成長率の押し下げ（前期比寄与度▲0.5％）は、国
家備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことによる公的在庫変動のプラス寄与（同 0.5％）によっ
てほぼ相殺されている。両者を合わせた成長率への影響はほぼ中立となった。 
５. マイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響が大きい
が、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が高い。
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにと
どまると予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-11-16

名目 GDPは前期比 0.4％（前期比年率 1.6％）と 10四半期連続で増加し、実質 GDPの伸びを上

となった。輸入デフレーターが前期比 3.3％となり、輸出デフレーターの伸び（同 1.3％）を上回っ
たことが GDPデフレーターを押し下げたが、国内需要デフレーターが前期比 1.0％（4-6月期：同
1.4％）の上昇となった。

### Page 3

＜需要項目別の動き＞ 
民間消費は、物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で、前期比▲0.3％と
2四半期連続で減少した。2026年 4-6月期が前期比 0.0％から同▲0.3％へ下方修正されたため、民
間消費の減少は 2四半期連続となった。 
3月末の自動車税の環境性能割廃止に伴う自動車販売の大幅増加や、2027年 4月の省エネ基準引
き上げを見越した駆け込み需要によるエアコン販売の好調により、4-6月期に大幅に増加した耐久
財が、その反動で大きく落ち込んだことも消費の下押し要因となった。 
実質家計消費の内訳を形態別にみると、外食、旅行、宿泊などのサービスは前期比 0.1％と小幅
な増加となったが、自動車、エアコンなどの耐久財が前期比▲3.5％の減少となったほか、値上げが
続く食料品などの非耐久財が同▲0.2％、被服・履物、家具などの半耐久財が同▲1.4％の減少とな
った。 
名目雇用者報酬は前年比 5.5％と、4-6月期の同 5.3％から伸びが高まった。実質雇用者報酬は前
年比 2.5％（4-6月期：同 2.6％）と 10四半期連続で増加した。

住宅投資は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算
値）は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万
戸から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸台
半ばで推移している。GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響もあり弱めの動きとなっている。

設備投資は前期比 1.6％と 3四半期ぶりに増加した。設備投資が高い伸びとなった一因は、4-6月
期に特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研究開発投資の減少によって落ち込んだ反動である。ただ、その影響
を除いても、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足に対応した省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情
報関連投資、AI・半導体関連などの成長分野への投資、Eコマースの拡大に伴う建設投資などを中
心に、回復基調が続いている。

公的需要は前期比 1.8％と 2四半期ぶりに増加した。高校授業料無償化や公立小学校の給食費無
償化に伴う家計消費支出の減少分が計上され、4-6月期に前期比 1.5％の高い伸びとなった政府消費
がその反動で同▲0.0％と減少し、公的固定資本形成は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。
一方、原油輸入量の急減を受けた国家備蓄原油の放出により前期比・寄与度▲0.5％の大幅マイナス
となった公的在庫変動が、備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことで、プラス寄与（同 0.5％）に転じた
ため、公的需要全体では前期比 1.8％の増加となった。

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。財貨・
サービスの輸出は前期比▲0.2％と 4四半期ぶりに減少した。半導体製造装置などの資本財輸出が好
調だったことから、財輸出は同 2.4％と増加したが、4-6月期に大幅に増加した研究開発サービスの

### Page 4

（2026年度後半は低空飛行が続く見通し） 
2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想してい
る。続く 2027年 1-3月期は、物価上昇率が 3％台まで高まることに加え、飲食料品の消費税率引き
下げを控えた買い控えも影響し、民間消費が減少する可能性が高く、実質 GDP成長率は前期比年
率 0％台前半にとどまるだろう。2026年度後半の日本経済は物価高による民間消費の低迷を主因と
して、低空飛行が続くことが予想される。

また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the
Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX
Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW
12 November 2026 | 3:15 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Post the historical open on 11/6/2026, we saw a jaw-dropping 14% climb from day-
low last Friday. Finally took a breather (and a big one too) today; admittedly, I was
writing a different narrative, but last hour fade in Taiwan has forced me to rewrite this

(>1k points drop and picked up market turnover). So, where do we go from here?
Check out below for my observation and investor feedback.
Key Investor Feedback and Takeaway
Sentiment. Sentiment is still cautious among foreigners; a lot of HFs faced serious
short squeeze from this rally. Yet, many are still reluctant to cover all their short
positions given the new short sell restrictions. and hence held on to the pain trades.
Interestingly, market still exhaust short sell quota almost every day. Additionally,

there is little conviction for a full-on rally and also low willingness to chase
around here given year-end. Overall, HFs have de-grossed much, one of the most
aggressive de-gross per GS prime book data. Key focus will be whether LOs are
willing to participate and to what extent will they add back the positions. Only
then, we will likely see a more sustainable rally further from here. Bottom line: are
we out of the woods? Not yet. Cautious optimism is perhaps the most positive

thing I can say for now.
Positioning. Foreigners have net sold for 10 straight sessions, totaling around
US$25.4bn. Most outflows are in TSMC but worth noting that we finally saw TSMC
net buy for the first time since 2 weeks yesterday. On the other hand, local funds
have been good size net buyers to absorb the foreign outflows, mainly from retail's
mutual fund subscription. Just regurgitating some of my berg blurbs from the past
few days, we still see lots of short interest in crowded long sectors, most in

ABF/CCL (i.e. NY Plastics) followed by CPO names; and most smid cap
outperformance was driven by short covering. The overly sold names saw little
attention and minimum desire to bottom fish (i.e. Delta, Chroma, KYEC). On a
brighter note, we also picked up some real money inflows into TSMC this week, but
I don't know whether it's enough to turn the tide. The more convicted long right
now for foreigners is still ASE in my opinion.
Retail. This is perhaps the most important factor now in Taiwan. Coupled with the

sentiment boost from National Stabilization Fund, many retail decided to hold
instead of cutting loss and even bought into the dip. For those retail that were
facing margin call during this correction, some were eventually saved by the market
rally. Cleaner positioning for sure, though perhaps not clean enough to fuel another
11/12/26, 3:20 PM Sales Trader Commentary: Squeezing the Shorts, Fading the Rallies; Navigating TAIEX Chaos Before Heading to the Vice City | GSTW

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/dc88e4dd-47de-45c1-bbf3-31c2c5c57299 1/11

### Page 2

10% climb in index. Per my local investor feedback, the smart pundits actually
many have missed out on this rally (of course they've also de-grossed much ahead).
Margin loan balance and maintenance ratio are two key data that I will follow
closely in the coming weeks.
Fundamentals. Fundamentals are still good with most company reporting record
high quarterly earnings. Monthly revenue also grew MoM. But stock trading is all
about forward looking. People are anticipating AI capex growth to slow down or

even plateau in the coming 2 years. Quanta (2382 TT) earnings call yesterday
validated this narrative - still high growth in 2027 but unlikely to see another triple-
digits rapid growth like 2026. Earnings outlooks probably matter less than
positioning and macro backdrop. With that, we will conclude most of our 3Q26
earnings this week with Hon Hai (2317 TT) as the main finale.
Liquidity Event: MSCI just published their quarterly review, and NYPCB (8046 TT)

and TUC (6274 TT) will be added into the index, while Lotes (3533 TT), Chailease
(5871 TT), Wan Hai (2615 TT), an E-Ink (8069 TT) will be removed. Kinsus (3189
TT) did not get added due to its disposition status. See below and this report for
our flows estimate.
Risk. Risk reward level here is not attractive, especially when the rebound came so
aggressively. Momentum to sustain the rally is not constructive, so investors should
be cautious of more volatility and further unwind (if there's anything to be

unwound). Jensen and Nvidia's headlines are certainly not helping, though I did see
some funny memes on the social media. Credit is another risk here; neoclouds top
line is +ve but let's not forget at what cost are these companies carving those
revenue from. Otherwise, market seems to process the macro prints well, with now
expecting a rate hike in Dec'26 and Mar'27. Last but not least, cross-strait tension
can be a focus next during Trump-Xi meeting at the APEC Summit next week. I

know the Taiwanese has been (probably will be) indifferent and just another BAU,
but anything assertively negative comment on Taiwan might fret the macro
traders, such as Xi's comment just now.
My View. Not a BEAR so far. I do not think the market is broken, but I
acknowledge the downside risk has risen again and enlarged as I respect the
technicals. That said, I am still excited about next week, even though we have fewer
fundamental catalysts now. In the US, Nvidia is set to report next week. A lighter

book might fare better in today's environment. To end on a fun personal note, GTA
VI will finally come out after 13 years of waiting. So if you can't find me on
Bloomberg, try looking for me in the Vice City.

inverse ETFs and ETNs "reset" daily, meaning that they are designed to achieve their stated objectives on a daily basis. They will therefore
realize each day a loss or a gain which is a multiple (for leveraged ETFs and ETNs) or the inverse (for inverse ETFs and ETNs) of the

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

swaps, and other derivatives as well as non-investment-grade securities - give rise to substantial risk and are not available to nor suitable
for all investors. If you have any questions about whether you are eligible to enter into these transactions with Goldman Sachs, please

values of which are influenced by foreign currencies, effectively assume currency risk.

Editorial
Tactical plays within a diversified allocation 
Strategy summary: Our calls to dial down risk since early 
October have helped mitigate a bear market in semiconductors 
and the sharpest single-day post-mid-term election downturn in 
US equities in over three decades. Volatility has eased after the 
post-election spike, helping the IT sector partly recover. We see 
further upside in our opportunistic US IT and healthcare sector 
equity ideas. In bonds, we lock in gains on US inflation-
protected bonds as inflation nears a peak.

Navigating volatility. Almost five weeks ago, we cut our 
semiconductor segment equity exposure as market breadth 
narrowed ahead of the US election. Two weeks ago, we cut 
equities from Overweight to a core allocation, positioning for

mid-cycle market dynamics. Last week, as the US equity 
market volatility index VIX surged above 25, we partially dialled 
up risk with a tactical idea on the more diversified US IT sector. 
Staying tactical within a diversified allocation. Equity 
volatility following last week’s Democratic clean sweep in the 
US elections has subsided. Questions about the pace of AI data 
centre buildout are likely to linger until Democrats take office in 
in January. Nvidia’s turmoil adds uncertainty. We remain 
broadly diversified and balanced in our core allocation across 
assets, sector and regions, while seeking tactical opportunities. 
History suggests sharp selloffs are followed by near-term 
volatility, particularly during macroeconomic stress. However, 
the balance of historical outcomes has typically shifted strongly 
to the upside over a three- to six-month horizon. Besides, 
historically, US equity markets have delivered the strongest 12-
month returns after opposition clean sweeps in the mid-terms. 
Rotation driving US IT sector recovery: As volatility eases, 
our opportunistic US IT equities idea, launched a week ago, has 
performed well, despite some significantly negative company 
specific developments. Attractive valuations support further 
upside. Investors are rotating within IT from semiconductors, 
affected by concerns over the pace of AI data centre buildout 
and Nvidia’s troubles, towards software and hardware 
providers. The latter have strong cash flows and balance sheets 
and are likely to benefit from AI monetisation. Together, they 
account for more than 60% of the IT sector (see page 4).

Remain constructive on US healthcare. US healthcare 
earnings are projected to grow almost 23% in 2027, the second-
fastest among MSCI US sectors. We see scope for further 
upside in the sector as innovation underpins long-term earnings 
growth. With the market rotating away from concentrated AI 
exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical demand profile 
offers a compelling exposure as a diversifier (see page 5). 
Outside the US, Switzerland and India offer diversified 
returns. The Swiss equity market consists of 41% healthcare, 
19% financial services and 14% consumer staples, providing a 
defensive alternative to investors seeking to diversify away 
from tech-heavy exposure. The Indian equity market has been 
classified as an “anti-AI” trade. Historically narrow valuation 
premiums vs. EMs, low foreign investor positioning and c. 15% 
expected 2027 earnings growth make it an attractive diversifier. 
Strong jobs and inflation reports cement December Fed 
rate hike. The US economy added 165,000 jobs in October, 
more than twice the 70,000 consensus estimate. The 
unemployment rate fell to 4.1%. Meanwhile, headline and core 
inflation in October, at 0.4% and 0.3% m/m, respectively, 
exceeded expectations as elevated fuel prices, ‘techflation’ and 
earlier tariffs fed into directly exposed sectors, including food, 
utilities and transportation services. A healthy job market keeps 
the Fed focused on lowering inflation, cementing the prospect 
of a December rate hike, potentially followed by another in Q1 
2027. A strong economy and elevated interest rates support our 
overweight allocation to US financial sector equities. 
Locking in gains from our bullish US inflation-protected 
bonds idea. While near-term price pressures remain elevated, 
we expect inflation to ease with energy costs over the coming 
year. Republicans’ heavy losses in the US mid-term elections 
increase pressure on the Trump administration to end the Iran 
conflict. One-year US inflation expectations have slumped 
alongside oil prices. A potential slowdown in the pace of AI 
buildout is also likely to ease techflation. Meanwhile, Fed rate 
hikes should further tighten financial conditions, tempering 
price pressures. This disinflationary outlook makes the risk-
reward for US inflation-protected bonds less compelling. We 
close the idea, locking in a 4.4% gain (page 6). 
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank

WS Global CIO Office | 13 November 2026

What does history tell us about the aftermath of a single-
day sell-off of 4% or more in the S&P500 index?

Our view: The S&P500 fell 4.36% on 5 November 2026, a rare daily 
move of 3.8 standard deviations. Since 2009, there have been only

24 occasions when the index declined by 4% or more in a single

session. The immediate aftermath of such sell-offs has historically

been mixed. One-month returns ranged from around -15% to

+23%, with positive returns in 70% of cases. The weaker

outcomes were concentrated around periods of significant macro

stress, including the tail end of the 2008 Global Financial Crisis

(GFC), the 2020 COVID-19 pandemic and the 2022 monetary policy

tightening cycle. 
The historical picture becomes considerably more constructive

beyond the first month. All 24 episodes generated positive three-
month returns, ranging from roughly +0.5% to +38%, while six-
month returns were positive in 96% of cases, with gains reaching 
as much as +49%. Overall, history suggests sharp sell-offs can be 
followed by near-term volatility, particularly during periods of broader 
macro stress. However, the balance of historical outcomes has 
shifted strongly to the upside over a 3-6-month horizon. 
—  Maggie Au Yeung, Quantitative Analyst 
 o
— Francis Lim, Senior Quantitative Strategist
Where do you see the most favourable risk-reward within

the US technology sector? 
Our view: We favour high-quality Big Tech companies with 
strong balance sheets and prefer diversified exposure through 
our US Information Technology (IT) opportunistic idea. 
Rationale: The recent pullback in US semiconductor stocks has 
improved their risk-reward profile. Earnings for these companies are 
forecast to grow by 65% in 2027, the fastest rate among technology 
subsectors. Despite this outlook, recent regulatory and corporate 
governance concerns are weighing on sentiment. Potential state-
level restrictions on data centre development following the recent 
US mid-term election results could weigh on semiconductor demand 
growth. However, we believe these risks are manageable and 
continued AI adoption should support demand for AI inference. We 
prefer diversified exposure to quality semiconductor 
companies with durable demand and strong volume growth. 
To navigate volatility in the US technology sector, we favour Big Tech 
companies with strong balance sheets and resilient cash generation, 
as they are better positioned to sustain AI investments without 
relying heavily on external financing. Our US IT opportunistic idea 
combines these preferences through exposure to semiconductors, 
software and hardware, allowing investors to participate in a 
potential recovery across a broader range of earnings drivers.
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d)

Do fundamental drivers support a constructive view on the 
US healthcare sector?

Our view: We remain constructive on US healthcare, supported

by its defensive characteristics and robust earnings growth.

Rationale: US healthcare earnings are projected to grow by 22.5% 
in 2027, the second-fastest among MSCI US sectors, driven by 
improving biopharma earnings. The US healthcare sector's 
valuation gap against the broader market has been recovering from 
a steep historical discount a year ago. The sector's forward price-to-

earnings (P/E) ratio of c. 20x now exceeds the broader US market’s

c. 18.4x, but we see scope for the valuation to be supported amid

investor demand for defensive growth. The sector’s broad legislative

policy overhang has materially faded following the US election cycle,

while targeted regulatory and oversight risks remain on the watchlist.

Healthcare innovation also underpins structural growth. Wider

adoption of obesity and diabetes treatments, alongside advances in

oncology treatments, medical devices and robotic surgery, should

support long-term earnings growth. With the market rotating away

from concentrated AI exposure, healthcare's stable, non-cyclical 
demand profile presents compelling opportunities for investors 
seeking both defensiveness and growth in the near term.
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst
What is your outlook for China’s Big Tech sector? 
Our view: We remain positive on China’s Big Tech sector, 
consistent with our Overweight stance on the country’s IT and 
communication services sector. 
Rationale: Valuations across China’s technology sector remain 
inexpensive at 14.2x, with investor attention centred on whether AI-
driven revenue can offset elevated AI-related spending, which is 
currently compressing margins. Elevated AI capex and negative 
free cash flow remain key investor concerns in the Q3 earnings 
season. Top-line momentum also remains uneven: soft domestic 
consumption continues to weigh on e-commerce, advertising and 
local services, whereas gaming and cloud computing are 
delivering resilient growth. The broader narrative is steadily 
transitioning towards commercialisation, with rapid enterprise 
penetration by AI workplace agents and upcoming AI model 
launches shifting the market focus towards active deployment and 
conversion to paid usage. Evolving regulatory frameworks 
covering AI governance, agent self-regulation, tax scrutiny and 
compliance requirements remain important variables influencing 
medium-term margin trajectories. Ultimately, valuation support, 
gradual monetisation of AI capabilities and resilient core 
segments support our Overweight positioning.
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Why are you closing your US Treasury Inflation-Protected

Securities (TIPS) opportunistic idea? 
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish US TIPS 
position, as the risk-reward profile has become less compelling.

The trade returned 4.4% since its initiation on 11 April 2025.

Rationale: We initiated an opportunistic bullish position in US TIPS

on 11 April 2025 as an oil and inflation hedge, expecting geopolitical

uncertainty to keep inflation expectations elevated. The risk-reward,

however, has since become less compelling, as hawkish Fed 
commentary, easing geopolitical risks and a potential slowdown in 
the pace of AI buildout are now likely to ease broader inflation and 
‘techflation’ pressures. We have therefore closed the opportunistic

idea, realising a gain of 4.4%.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist
How do falling oil prices and October’s US inflation data 
affect your Fed and bond market outlook? 
Our view: Our base case envisages two further 25bps Fed hikes 
by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 2027 if energy prices 
remain consistently lower and growth trajectory normalises. We 
favour income over chasing duration gains, focusing on higher-
quality corporate bonds with 3-7-year maturities. 
Rationale: Lower oil prices have a two-sided effect: they ease 
inflationary pressures and support growth by boosting purchasing 
power and lowering business costs. Cheaper energy, therefore, 
does not automatically justify Fed easing. Our base case envisages 
two further 25bps hikes by mid-2027, with scope for cuts in H2 
2027 if energy prices remain consistently lower. Sustained 
moderation in core inflation would challenge the near-term tightening 
path. However, October’s US consumer inflation print reinforced our 
assessment that each Fed meeting now remains a ‘live’ decision. 
With the US 10-year government bond yield already within our 5.0-
5.25% 3-month range, we favour income over chasing duration 
gains. Credit performance has diverged: Developed Market high-
yield bonds have outperformed both investment-grade credit and 
government bonds. Credit still offers attractive income, and we retain 
our preference for 3-7-year maturities, although heavy primary 
issuance remains a counterweight. We favour selective carry, with a 
preference for higher-quality corporate bonds. These include bonds 
issued by leading AI hyperscalers, where risk-reward has become 
more attractive. Our emphasis is on generating steady income, 
rather than relying on further declines in bond yields or spreads.
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d)
Does the USD’s rally have further to run?

Our view: Further upside appears limited. Stronger-than-

expected US core inflation in October and hawkish Fed

comments have pushed the US Dollar Index (DXY) above 103.

However, markets already price in more Fed tightening than we

expect. We see the DXY returning to its 101.00-102.95 range in

the near term, before gradually weakening towards our 12-

month forecast of 98.5.

Rationale: US core consumer prices rose 0.3% m/m in October,

above the 0.2% m/m expected, meeting Fed Governor Waller’s

threshold for considering a 50bps December rate hike. This pushed

US government bond yields higher, supporting the USD rally. 
However, futures now imply a Fed rate peak of 4.50-4.75% by

mid-2027, compared with our expectation of 4.25-4.50% following

two 25bps hikes, one in December and the second in H1 2027. We

expect the Fed to begin easing in H2 2027. As market expectations 
gradually converge towards our view, USD support from US bond 
yields should weaken.
Meanwhile, falling oil and petrol prices should help ease headline 
inflation in the coming months, reducing pressure for further Fed 
tightening. This reinforces our expectation of limited USD upside, 
although persistent services inflation remains a key risk.
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist
Do you expect USD/JPY to sustainably break out above 
160 in the near term?
Our view: We see USD/JPY rangebound with a downside bias, 
as growing BoJ tightening expectations and potential policy 
convergence will support gradual JPY appreciation.
Rationale: Renewed tech optimism has encouraged the re-
establishment of carry trades, providing support to the pair lately. 
Meanwhile, US core inflation beat expectations for a third 
consecutive month in October, reinforcing expectations for a 
December Fed hike. Further upside inflation surprises could widen 
the US-Japan interest rate differential and push USD/JPY higher. 
However, the risk of intervention by Japan’s Ministry of Finance 
is likely to rise materially around 160, limiting the scope for 
sustained upside. On the downside, a 25bps BoJ hike to 1.50%, 
accompanied by clear signals of consecutive hikes in March and 
June 2027, would be a significant yen-positive catalyst. A more non-
linear downside scenario would be a disorderly equity sell-off, which 
could trigger rapid JPY appreciation through carry-trade unwinding. 
We see scope for the pair to test support towards 156 in the 
coming weeks.
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist
